Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the biophysics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 691. The resulting operator achieves bound O(173/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biophysics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^691 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 173-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
